<div class="product-single__description rte">
<p>Designed by Ryan Connolly, the Triune Sound: Hits and Risers pack comes with over 170 sounds to create any impact sound you need for your film. </p>

<li>21 basic hits that can fit into any music track to boost the moment or create your own sound.</li>
<li>27 riser sounds to blend and build.</li>
<li>2 bonus horror underscores. </li>
</ul>
